END OF REPORT
SLC measures goaltending as a service to the defensive unit.
Stabilizer: above league average SLC and above league average Sovereignty.
Systems Man: above league average SLC and below league average Sovereignty.
Functional Drain: below league average SLC and above league average Sovereignty.
Systemic Drain: below league average SLC and below league average Sovereignty.
Sovereignty measures shot quality control (actual SV% minus expected SV%).
Reset Ratio measures saves per defensive sequence.
A positive Net Load means the goalie is a net asset to their team's energy.
A negative Net Load means the goalie is costing their team more than they are giving back.
